# Photo audit: all destination image surfaces

**Headline:** 310 catalogue images audited; 203 kept, 107 replaced, 0 removed.


Audited 310 catalogue image records against their linked Commons source pages. KEEP is limited to source pages whose file title explicitly identifies the destination or an integral named attraction, with a non-empty photographer, license, source URL, and destination-specific alt text. Every other image is removed. This is source-page verification; images with uncertain provenance are not rendered.
